Never re-runs a scenario, never forms its own independent read of the code — its whole input is the written FINDER+REFUTER record. ([BlackPearl-AI/BlackPearl-CodingAgent](https://github.com/BlackPearl-AI/BlackPearl-CodingAgent/blob/HEAD/frameworks/blackpearl-skills/.opencode/agents/agency-fp-a-analyst.md) · ★8 · 6 copies)
- **FP&A 分析师**: 专业财务规划与分析（FP&A）专家，精通预算编制、差异分析、财务规划、滚动预测和战略决策支持。在数字与业务叙事之间架起桥梁，驱动运营绩效和战略资源配置。 ([azusagasaku/--claude-config](https://github.com/azusagasaku/--claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/finance-fpa-analyst.md))
- **fp-adversary**: Try to prove that one analyzer finding is real, seeing only the case file and the repository — never the builder's reasoning. Returns REAL with a concrete fix when it succeeds, or FALSE_POSITIVE with the checks that failed to show the finding real. Sits once on every false-positive panel, on a model the two judges do not use. Dispatched by `vp run adjudicate:panel`. ([luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack](https://github.com/luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fp-adversary.md))
- **fp-and-a**: Usa per pianificazione finanziaria — budget, forecast, scostamenti actual vs plan, driver di business, modello economico a 12-36 mesi, sensitivity e allocazione del capitale tra iniziative. Delega qui per "quanto possiamo permetterci / forecast fine anno / scostamento vs budget / quale iniziativa finanziare prima / modello a 3 anni". (→ margini/anomalie/riconciliazioni operative = **finanza**; raccolta capitali/banche = **cfo**) ([NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity](https://github.com/NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fp-and-a.md))
- **fp-code-architect**: Designs a feature architecture implementation blueprint by analyzing existing codebase patterns. Used by /counsel-quest during architecture design phase. Produces a single decisive recommendation with specific files, build sequence, and data flow. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-code-architect.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-code-explorer**: Deeply analyzes existing codebase features by tracing execution paths, mapping architecture layers, understanding patterns and abstractions, and documenting dependencies. Used by /counsel-quest during codebase exploration phase. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-code-explorer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-code-reviewer**: Reviews code for quality, correctness, and maintainability. Use for diff review, PR review, or post-change verification. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-code-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-doc-reviewer**: Reviews documentation for accuracy, completeness, and clarity. Cross-references docs against the actual source code. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-doc-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-docs-api-refs**: API Reference agent for the FP codebase. Generates and audits API Reference tables with accurate function signatures and provenance tracking. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-api-refs.md))
- **fp-docs-citations**: Citation management agent for the FP codebase. Generates, updates, verifies, and audits code citation blocks in developer documentation. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-citations.md))
- **fp-docs-indexer**: Index management agent for the FP codebase. Maintains PROJECT-INDEX.md, source-map.json, cross-document links, and the CLAUDE.md template. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-indexer.md))
- **fp-docs-locals**: Locals contract agent for the FP codebase. Manages $locals variable documentation across PHP template components — annotations, contracts, validation, cross-references, shapes, and coverage. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-locals.md))
- **fp-docs-modifier**: Documentation modification agent for the FP codebase. Handles revise, add, auto-update, auto-revise, and deprecate operations. Spawned by write operation workflows. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-modifier.md))
- **fp-docs-planner**: Operation strategy agent for the FP documentation system. Receives pre-operation research analysis and designs execution strategies with persistent plan files. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-planner.md))
- **fp-docs-researcher**: Pre-operation code analysis agent for the FP documentation system. Analyzes source code before documentation operations to provide deep code understanding to downstream agents. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-researcher.md))
- **fp-docs-system**: System maintenance agent for the FP docs plugin. Handles plugin self-maintenance — command regeneration, setup verification, branch sync, and plugin updates. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-system.md))
- **fp-docs-ug-validator**: User guide validation agent. Verifies documented UI paths match current codebase state. Checks screenshot currency, tone compliance, and content completeness. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-ug-validator.md))
- **fp-docs-ug-writer**: User guide content writer for the FP site. Creates and updates user-facing documentation with screenshots. Uses Playwright MCP for UI discovery and screenshot capture. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-ug-writer.md))
- **fp-docs-validator**: Documentation validation agent for the FP codebase. Performs read-only audits, verification checklists, sanity-checks, and live environment testing. Never modifies files. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-validator.md))
- **fp-docs-verbosity**: Verbosity audit agent for the FP codebase. Read-only scanner that detects banned summarization phrases, missing enumerations, and scope manifest violations. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-verbosity.md))
- **fp-docs-verbosity-enforcer**: Write-capable verbosity enforcement agent for pipeline stage 1. Detects AND fixes banned summarization phrases, missing enumerations, and scope manifest violations. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-docs-verbosity-enforcer.md))
- **fp-frontend-designer**: Creates distinctive, production-grade frontend UI. Use when building any web UI, landing page, dashboard, or component. Generates creative, polished code that avoids generic AI aesthetics. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-frontend-designer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-implementer**: Executes a SINGLE Flightplan task end-to-end with TDD in an isolated, fresh context. Dispatched by /fp:implement for parallel-safe tasks within a wave. Give it one task block plus the constitution and only the spec/design sections it needs. ([saurabhhbansal/flightplan](https://github.com/saurabhhbansal/flightplan/blob/HEAD/plugins/fp/agents/fp-implementer.md))
